# Linux — inferred

The **7,859** projects that probably run on Linux but do not say so. Each verdict here comes from the install route or the language runtime, never from a release asset or from the README — that is what puts them on this page rather than with the [3,043 confirmed ones](linux.md).

Read `Why` before trusting a row. A portable runtime is a good bet and not a promise.

[← Linux](linux.md) · [← back to the mega list](../README.md)

Page **7** of 11, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](linux-inferred.md) · [2](linux-inferred-2.md) · [3](linux-inferred-3.md) · [4](linux-inferred-4.md) · [5](linux-inferred-5.md) · [6](linux-inferred-6.md) · **7** · [8](linux-inferred-8.md) · [9](linux-inferred-9.md) · [10](linux-inferred-10.md) · [11](linux-inferred-11.md) — [continue on page 8 →](linux-inferred-8.md)

The website is the maintained edition. Its last workbooks: [dark](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-DARK.xlsx) · [light](https://github.com/crazy54/awesome-agentic-atlas/releases/download/v2026.10.22/Awesome-Agentic-Atlas-LIGHT.xlsx).
